Consider a_i = 1 + d * (-1)^i (alternating 1+d, 1-d). Nonnegative if d≤1. Let n even. Let's compute convolution for periodic two-state. For infinite sequence, convolution full? Finite length. For a_i = A if even, B if odd. Sum ≈ n(A+B)/2. Convolution R[k]=∑ a_i a_{k-i}. For a given k, product is AB if parities differ, A^2 or B^2 if same. Number of terms ~k+1 minus boundary. If A+B constant (normalize mean 1), A=1+t, B=1-t. For interior k, R[k] ≈ (#same) * ((1+t)^2+(1-t)^2)/2? Actually count of pairs with same parity vs diff parity.

For full convolution of alternating sequence length n, at center, number of terms n. Among i=0..n-1, parity of i and k-i: if k even, same parity; if k odd, diff parity. So for even k, sum ≈ n*(A^2+B^2)/2? If k even, all pairs i and k-i have same parity? If k even, i parity = k-i parity, so product A^2 for both even or B^2 for both odd. Number even i ~n/2, odd ~n/2. Sum ≈ (n/2)A^2+(n/2)B^2 = n/2(A^2+B^2)=n/2*2(1+t^2)=n(1+t^2). This is larger than uniform n for t>0. For odd k: products all AB, sum≈n(1-t^2). What about 3-phase periodic? Nonnegative weights p_j for residues mod 3. Convolution count depends on residue sums. For large n, interior R[k] ≈ (n/3)* average over residues (p_r p_{s} with r+s ≡ k mod3). We can choose p to minimize max over k residues. This is a cyclic convolution on Z_3 scaled by n/3. Sum p=1? Period m: For any periodic distribution of period m, the asymptotic max of cyclic convolution C_m(q) has sum 1 over m lags, so max≥1/m. The score asymptotic = 2n * (n/m * maxC) / (n^2 * 1^2?) Let's design final code carefully:
```
import time, math, numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution, minimize

def _score_from_p(p):
    n=len(p); S=p.sum(); if S<1e-12: return inf
    p=p/S
    c=np.convolve(p,p)
    M=c.max()
    return 2*n*M
```
Since sum=1 exactly after normalization, score =2n*max(c). evaluator score same.

For fitness raw:
```
def make_raw_fitness(n):
 def f(x):
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   x=np.clip(x,0,None) # upper maybe 1 not necessary; after normalization scale irrelevant; but if huge? fine.
   S=x.sum()
   if S<1e-12: return 1e100
   return _score_from_p(x/S)
 return f
```

Softmax fitness:
```
def softmax(y):
  y=y-y.max(); e=np.exp(y); return e/e.sum()
def fit_softmax(y,n): return _score_from_p(softmax(y))
```

Implement exact objective with gradient for softmax:
```
def softmax(y): ...
def grad_p_c_k(p,k,n):
    # gradient wrt p: 2 * p[k - i] if 0<=k-i<n else 0, for vectorized:
    g=np.zeros(n)
    # We need for each i, j=k-i. So g[i]=2*p[j] if 0<=j<n.
    # vectorize: for j in range(n): i=k-j; if 0<=i<n: g[i]=2*p[j]
    for j in range(n):
       i=k-j
       if 0<=i<n: g[i]=2*p[j]
    return g
```
Then for active set, gradient of max = sum_{a in active} grad(c_a) / len(active) (if using average) times maybe. Actually max of functions: subgradient convex hull of gradients of active. Use average for direction.
Then grad_y = J^T grad_p, where J = diag(p) - outer(p,p). So grad_y = p * grad_p - p * (p @ grad_p). Good.
F = 2n*max(c). Gradient = F/M * average grad_p? If M>0. If using average gradient of c_k, ∂max = avg ∂c_k (one subgradient). Multiply 2n. So grad = 2n * avg grad_p.

Smooth objective:
```
def smooth_score(y,n,beta):
 p=softmax(y); c=np.convolve(p,p); c=np.clip(c,1e-300,None)
 logc=np.log(c)
 # logsumexp stable
 m=logc.max(); lse=m+math.log(np.sum(np.exp(logc-m)))
 return 2*n*(lse/beta)
```
Gradient can be computed by finite differences (n small) or analytic with softmax Jacobian. For smooth max, weights w_k = softmax(beta logc). grad_p(c_k)=2p_{k-i}; grad_p = ∑ w_k grad_p(c_k). grad_y = p*grad_p - p*(p@grad_p). Analytic feasible.
Let's implement analytic for speed.
```
def smooth_fit(y,n,beta):
    p=softmax(y)
    c=np.convolve(p,p)
    c=np.maximum(c,1e-300)
    logc=np.log(c)
    w=softmax(beta*logc) # shape 2n-1
    # grad_p zeros
    grad_p=np.zeros(n)
    for k,wk in enumerate(w):
       if wk==0: continue
       # add wk*2*p[k-i]
       # i from max(0,k-n+1) to min(n-1,k)
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       # j=k-i ranges from k-lo to k-hi, reversed. Better loop j in range(n): i=k-j
       for j in range(n):
          i=k-j
          if 0<=i<n:
             grad_p[i]+=2*wk*p[j]
    val=2*(n/beta)*(max_log + logsum...)
    # grad_y
    r = p @ grad_p
    gy = p*(grad_p - r)
    return val, gy
```
Loop over all k and j O(n^2) fine. Could vectorize but not needed.
Need ensure for beta large, w softmax may under/overflow; use stable softmax on z=beta*(logc-maxlog). Good.

Function `score_p_full(p)`:
```
p = np.asarray(p,float)
p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0)
p = np.clip(p,0,None)
S=p.sum()
if S <= 1e-12: return 1e100
p /= S
c = np.convolve(p,p)
M = float(np.max(c))
return float(2*len(p)*M)
```
For p sum1, score. If p contains NaN from y? handle.

`softmax(y)`:
```
y = np.clip(np.asarray(y,float), -50, 50)
y = y - np.max(y)
e = np.exp(y)
s = e.sum()
if s==0: return np.full_like(y, 1/len(y))
return e/s
```
If y contains nan, replace.

```
def fit_y(y,n):
   y=np.asarray(y,float)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): return 1e100
   p=softmax(y)
   return score_p_sum1(p) # p sum1
```

Smooth fitness with gradient for L-BFGS. Let's implement because it can refine quickly.
```
def smooth_fit_y(y,n,beta):
   y=finite clip
   p=softmax(y)
   c=np.convolve(p,p)
   c=np.clip(c, 1e-300, None)
   logc=np.log(c)
   z=beta*(logc-logc.max())
   ez=np.exp(z); w=ez/ez.sum()
   # value
   lse = logc.max() + math.log(ez.sum())
   val = 2.0*n*(lse/beta)
   # grad_p
   grad_p=np.zeros(n)
   # vectorized possible:
   # for each k: grad_p += 2*w[k]* p_rev? where i=k-j -> grad_p[i] += 2w[k]p[j].
   # We can loop j:
   for j in range(n):
      pj=p[j]
      if pj==0: continue
      kmin = max(0,j)??? For each j (partner index), i=k-j must in [0,n-1], so k ranges j to j+n-1. For each k in that range, w[k] contributes 2*w[k]*pj to grad_p[i=k-j]? Wait grad_p[i] gets from partner j = k-i. For fixed j, i=k-j; as k varies, i varies. We can loop i instead.
   # Simpler double loop:
   for k,wk in enumerate(w):
      if wk==0: continue
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      # i in lo..hi, j=k-i
      # grad_p[i] += 2*wk*p[j]
      # vector: i=np.arange(lo,hi+1); j=k-i
      i = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
      grad_p[i] += 2*wk*p[k - i]
   gp = grad_p
   r = float(p.dot(gp))
   gy = p*(gp - r)
   return val, gy
```
`w` length 2n-1. For n small fine. Clipping c >0.
But if beta large, w might be one-hot on max; still smooth-ish. L-BFGS-B with this gradient for beta e.g. 100. Good.

We need avoid high beta causing underflow in ez; stable.

Local routine:
```
def refine_y(y0,n, max_time=None):
   y=np.clip(y0,-50,50)
   best_y=y; best_f=fit_y(y,n)
   # Smooth L-BFGS for beta in [20,100,500]
   for beta in (20,100,500):
       if timeout: break
       res=minimize(lambda yy: smooth_fit_y(yy,n,beta)[0], y, jac=lambda yy: smooth_fit_y(yy,n,beta)[1], method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':500,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8})
       if res.fun<best_f:
          y=res.x; best_f=fit_y(y)
   # Exact Powell
   res=minimize(lambda yy: fit_y(yy,n), y, method='Powell', options={'maxiter':1000,'maxfev':10000})
   if res.fun<best_f: y=res.x; best_f=res.fun
   # Try Nelder-Mead maybe
   res=minimize(fit_y, y, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':2000,'xatol':1e-12,'fatol':1e-12})
   ...
   return y,best_f
```
Could be redundant. Powell may be enough. L-BFGS with smooth gradient faster.

FFT convolution:
```
def convolve_fft(a):
   n=len(a)
   N=1
   while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   fb=fa*fa
   c=np.fft.irfft(fb,N)[:2*n-1]
   # due to floating errors, real nonnegative-ish
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   return c
```
For n=4498, N=16384? 2n-1=8995, next pow2 16384. Good.

Let's outline large optimize:
```
def optimize_large(n, init, iterations=None):
   p=normalize(init)
   best_p=p; best_score=score_fft(p)
   step_factor=1.0
   no_improve=0
   for it in range(iterations):
      if timeout: break
      c=conv_fft(p)
      M=c.max()
      K=min(len(c), max(50, n//20)) # e.g., n=1024 K=51
      # get weights topK
      idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
      beta = 50.0 / max(M,1e-12)
      z=beta*(c[idx]-M); w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
      g=np.zeros(n)
      for wk,k in zip(w,idx):
          if wk < 1e-6: continue
          lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
          seg = a? p? grad uses current p
          g[lo:hi+1] += 2*wk*p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
      # normalize gradient maybe
      gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if gn==0: break
      base = p.max()/gn? or p.mean()/gn? Since p mean=1/n, pmax~a few/n. Use p.mean()/gn.
      # Try coefficients [0.5,1,2,5,10,20] * base maybe.
      accepted=False
      for coeff in [0.05,0.1,0.3,1,3,10,30,100]:
          q = p - coeff*base*g
          # keep nonnegative with floor
          q = np.maximum(q, 0)
          # optional add small floor: q = np.maximum(q, 1e-12)
          S=q.sum()
          if S <=0: continue
          q/=S
          sc=score_fft(q)
          if sc < best_score:
              best_p=q; best_score=sc; p=q; accepted=True; break
      if accepted:
          no_improve=0
          step_factor = min(100, step_factor*1.1)
      else:
          no_improve+=1
          step_factor = max(1e-3, step_factor*0.5)
          if no_improve>300: break
   return best_p,best_score
```

Let's design final code with multiple strategies, prioritizing reliability:

Let's write helper functions:
```
def _direct_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<0.01: return inf
    # Since evaluator clips max 1000; if a large? We output normalized sum1 so no issue.
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    M= b.max()
    return float(2*a.size*M/S**2)
```
For internal normalized full convolution, if using FFT:
```
def _fft_score(a):
   n=a.size
   N=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<1e-12: return inf
   # a may not sum1; use formula scaled: score=2n max(c)/S².
   return float(2*n*c.max()/(S*S))
```
Works for any scale. For normalized sum1 S=1.
Potential FFT error for large values? We keep p sum1, fine.

For topK gradient using a not normalized? We'll maintain p sum1. If using score formula with S, but gradient should for normalized? Objective scale invariant; we can use p with sum1.

LP matrix construction efficient:
```
def make_conv_matrix(f):
   n=len(f); m=2*n-1
   A=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=float)
   # For each k, valid j range: lo=max(0,k-n+1), hi=min(n-1,k)
   # A[k, j] = f[k-j]
   # This can be built by for j in range(n): i = k-j? Alternatively for i in range(n): for k? We need dense assignment.
   for j in range(n):
       # i ranges such that k=i+j from i to i+n-1? For column j, A[k, j]=f[k-j] if 0≤k-j<n.
       # So for i in 0..n-1, k=i+j; A[i+j, j] = f[i]
       A[np.arange(j, j+n), j] = f[np.arange(n)]
   return A
```

```
def search_small(max_n=8):
  for n in [6,5,4,7,3,2,8]:
    if deadline low break
    # DE on y
    for seed in range(3):
       res=differential_evolution(lambda y: fit_y(y,n), bounds=[(-8,8)]*n, seed=seed+n, maxiter=200, popsize=150, polish=False)
       consider_global(softmax(res.x), True)
       # Powell refine
       res2=minimize(fit_y, res.x, method='Powell', options={'maxiter':1000,'maxfev':10000})
       consider_global(softmax(res2.x), True)
    # random starts
    for _ in range(30):
       y=rng.normal(size=n)*1.0
       res=minimize(fit_y, y, method='Powell', options={'maxiter':500,'maxfev':5000})
       consider_global(...)
    # support masks for n<=6:
    if n<=6:
       for mask in range(1,1<<n):
          bits=[i for i in range(n) if mask>>i&1]
          m=len(bits)
          if m<2: continue
          # analytic m=2? skip or optimize
          # start uniform on mask
          y0=rng.normal(size=m)*0.1
          res=minimize(fit_mask, y0, method='Powell', options={'maxfev':3000})
          consider_global(p_full, direct)
```

LP matrix building optimization: For each row k, we can assign slice:
```
A = np.empty((2*n-1, n), dtype=float) # initialize zero? Need zero. Use zeros.
for k in range(2*n-1):
    lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
    hi = min(n - 1, k)
    if lo <= hi:
        j = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
        i = k - j
        A[k, j] = f[i]
```
Loop over rows (2n) with vector lengths average n/2, total n^2. For n=1024, okay. Or column loop as above:
```
A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
idx=np.arange(n)
for j in range(n):
    A[j:j+n, j] = f  # because k = i+j, i 0..n-1, coefficient f[i]
```
This is simpler and efficient. For n=1024, loop 1024, each assignment length1024 ->1M. Good. A[k,j]=f[k-j] since i=k-j, i from0 to n-1 as k from j to j+n-1. Yes.

Line search for LP:
```
def trial_direction(current, q):
    g_sum=q.sum(); if g_sum<=0 return None
    q=q/g_sum
    best=None
    for t in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,0.02,0.15,0.35):
       p_new=(1-t)*current + t*q
       sc=score_fft(p_new)
       if best is None or sc<best_sc: ...
    # Also try t values scaled if no improvement? maybe.
    return best_p,best_sc
```

Exponentiated gradient detailed:
```
def mirror_optimize(p0, n, iters=2000, K_ratio=0.05, beta_factor=50, time_limit=60):
 p=normalize(p0)
 best=p copy, best_sc=score_direct? use fft
 step=1.0
 no=0
 for it in range(iters):
   if timeout: break
   c=conv_fft(p); M=c.max()
   K=min(len(c), max(16, int(len(c)*K_ratio)))
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   w=softmax(beta_factor*(c[idx]-M)/max(M,1e-12))
   g=np.zeros(n)
   for wk,k in zip(w,idx):
      if wk < 1e-7: continue
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      g[lo:hi+1] += 2*wk*p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
   gm=np.abs(g).max()
   if gm<1e-15: break
   r=g/gm
   accepted=False
   # try eta list scaled by step
   for eta in (0.05*step,0.1*step,0.3*step,1*step,3*step,10*step,30*step):
      q=p*np.exp(-eta*r)
      q=np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0)
      q=np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
      S=q.sum(); if S<=0: continue
      q/=S
      sc=score_fft(q)
      if sc<best_sc:
         best=q; best_sc=sc; p=q; accepted=True; break
   if accepted:
      no=0; step=min(50, step*1.1)
   else:
      no+=1; step=max(0.01, step*0.5)
      if no>200: break
 return best,best_sc
```
Note `best_sc` global? For a run. We can call with current best.
Use K_ratio increasing over phases? We can call mirror with K_ratio=0.01,0.03,0.10. Or set K = max(64, n//20). For n=1024 K=64. Good.

But mirror update with r=g/gm: if g all positive (maybe), exp(-eta*r) reduces high g coordinates; coordinates with g=0 unchanged; after normalization, increases lows. Good.

Potential issue: `g` for active lags may be positive everywhere? Since p nonnegative, each grad term positive where valid, zero outside. Average g nonnegative (all entries ≥0). No negative! Indeed g_i = sum weights 2 p[k-i] ≥0. So exp(-eta*r) only decreases coordinates, none increase directly. But after normalization, coordinates with smaller g decrease less (relative increase). Thus effective increase for low g. If some g=0, they are not decreased, relative increase. Uniform top-K central: endpoints g lower maybe zero for lags not covering, so endpoints increase. Good.

The update is always reducing a linear combination of active c? Since g nonnegative, derivative of top-K objective along q? likely.

Because g entries are O(1/n), r shape more important. Eta values dimensionless; exp(-30) underflows for high r=1; coordinates with r near1 become tiny. Step adaptation starts step=1. Eta 30 maybe too aggressive, but line search best of small/large. Good.

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize, differential_evolution

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
   deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1,budget_s) - 8.0
   rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
   best_list=[]; best_score=np.inf
   def tl(): return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
   def consider(a, direct=None):
      nonlocal best_score,best_list
      a=np.asarray(a,float)
      if a.size==0: return
      a=a.ravel()
      a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
      a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
      if a.sum() <= 0.01? For internal we normalize before considering, but if not, direct score inf if sum<0.01. We'll normalize:
      S=a.sum()
      if S <=1e-12: return
      a=a/S # This changes sum to1; score unchanged. But if original had values >1000? normalized below1.
      # direct score for update? Use direct if a.size <= 2048 or direct flag True; else fft? To be safe, direct for all best updates? length4498 direct okay.
      sc = _direct_score(a)
      if sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_list=a.copy()
```

Let's write LP optimizer function with time limit:
```
def run_lp(n, p0, max_iters, time_limit=None):
  p=normalize(p0); best=p.copy; best_sc=_fft_score(p)
  if tl() < 15 and time_limit? return
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
    sc=_fft_score(p); c=conv_fft(p); M=c.max()
    A=build_matrix(p)
    rhs=M*np.ones(2*n-1)
    try:
       res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=rhs, bounds=(0,1.0), method='highs', options={'TimeLimit': 20, 'presolve': True})
       if res.status not in (0, None) or not hasattr(res,'x'): g=p
       else:
          g=np.asarray(res.x,float); g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0); g=np.clip(g,0,1)
       if g.sum() <= 1e-8: g=p
       q=g/g.sum()
       # try t
       best_t=None
       for t in (0.2,0.5,0.8,0.1,0.35,1.0,0.05):
          p_new=(1-t)*p + t*q
          sc_new=_fft_score(p_new)
          if best_t is None or sc_new<best_t_sc: ...
       if best_t_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          p=best_t_p; best_sc=best_t_sc
          consider(p) # direct update
          no_improve=0
       else:
          # maybe try q with small random? or break
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve>20: break
    except Exception:e:
       # fallback to mirror? break
       break
  return best,best_sc
```
`linprog` TimeLimit: If LP exceeded, res may have status? HiGHS may return solution? We can still use if res.x. But if time limit 20 and many solves, could total exceed. We'll catch. Use `options={'TimeLimit': min(20, tl()-30)}`. But `TimeLimit` units seconds? yes. If no option, okay.

def quick_small():
  # n=2 exact
  consider([1,2])
  # n=4 known
  consider([1,1,2,1])
  # n=6: DE 1 seed + Powell from 20 random if time
  if tl()>30:
    for n in (6,5,4):
      if tl()<10: break
      y0=np.zeros(n)
      try:
         res=differential_evolution(lambda y: _fit_y(y,n), bounds=[(-6,6)]*n, seed=1, maxiter=100, popsize=100, polish=False)
         y=res.x
      except: y=y0
      for _ in range(15):
         yrand=rng.normal(size=n)*1
         try: resm=minimize(_fit_y, yrand, method='Powell', options={'maxiter':500,'maxfev':3000})
         except: resm=None
         if resm.fun < _fit_y(y): y=resm.x
      consider(softmax(y))
```

  def consider(a): ... uses _direct_score, updates

Let's code mirror nested:
```
def mirror_optimize(n, p0, iters):
    nonlocal best_score,best_list
    p=_normalize(p0)
    local_best=p.copy(); local_best_sc=_fft_score(p)
    scale=0.5; no=0
    for it in range(iters):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90: break
      c=_fft_conv(p); M=c.max()
      if M<=0: break
      K=min(c.size, max(32, n//8))
      idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
      beta=20.0/max(M,1e-15)
      z=beta*(c[idx]-M)
      w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
      g=np.zeros(n)
      for wk,k in zip(w,idx):
         if wk < 1e-7: continue
         lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
         if lo<=hi:
            g[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*wk*p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
      gm=np.abs(g).max()
      if gm < 1e-16: break
      improved=False
      # mirror trials
      for eta in (0.1*scale,0.3*scale,1.0*scale,3.0*scale,10.0*scale):
         eta=min(50.0, max(1e-3, eta))
         q=p*np.exp(-eta*(g/gm))
         q=np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
         q=np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
         S=q.sum()
         if S<=1e-12: continue
         q/=S
         sc=_fft_score(q)
         if sc < local_best_sc - 1e-13:
             local_best=q; local_best_sc=sc; p=q; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
         base=p.mean()/gm
         for coeff in (0.1*scale,0.5*scale,2.0*scale,10.0*scale):
            q=p - coeff*base*g
            q=np.where(np.isfinite(q), q,0.0)
            q=np.maximum(q,0.0); S=q.sum(); if S<=1e-12: continue
            q/=S; sc=_fft_score(q); if sc < local_best_sc...
      if improved:
         no=0; scale=min(10.0, scale*1.15)
         # global update
         if local_best_sc < best_score - 1e-12:
             scd=_direct_score(p)
             if scd < best_score:
                best_score=scd; best_list=p.copy()
      else:
         no+=1; scale=max(0.02, scale*0.6)
         if no>300: break
    # final direct
    scd=_direct_score(local_best)
    if scd < best_score: update
```
But note global `best_score` is exact direct, local_best_sc FFT. After mirror run, we do direct local_best even if not improved global. Good.
We update best only based on direct score, but use local FFT for run. If local_best_sc improves but direct slightly worse due error, global may not update; okay. At end direct.

LP nested:
```
def run_lp(n,p0,max_iters):
  p=_normalize(p0); local=p.copy; local_sc=_fft_score(p)
  no=0
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if tl()<150? break
    c=_fft_conv(p); M=c.max()
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
    for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=p
    rhs=M*np.ones(2*n-1)
    try:
      res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=rhs, bounds=[(0,1.0)]*n, method='highs', options={'TimeLimit': 20, 'output_flag':0})
      if res.status not (0,3?) use p
    except:
      try: res=linprog(..., method='highs')
      except: res=None
    if res is None or not res.success:
       g=p
    else:
       g=np.asarray(res.x); ...
    # To avoid pure delta from degenerate, blend g with p: g = 0.8*g + 0.2*p? This may help alternate.
    g = 0.85*g + 0.15*p; normalize
    qg=g/g.sum()
    # Try t
    imp=False
    for t in (0.2,0.5,0.1,0.35,0.8,1.0,0.05):
       pnew=(1-t)*p + t*qg
       sc=_fft_score(pnew)
       if sc < local_sc - 1e-13: local=pnew; local_sc=sc; p=pnew; imp=True; break
    if imp:
      no=0
      scd=_direct_score(p); if scd<best_score update
    else:
      no+=1; if no>30: break
  scd=_direct_score(local); if scd<best_score update
  return local
```
Blending g with p prevents spike and may smooth. But it changes LP direction; still okay heuristic. Use 0.8*LP+0.2*p maybe. If LP returns delta, blend keeps broad. Good.
Line search t values: if LP direction bad due blend, maybe no improve; break.
Potential issue: The LP maximizes sum g; if we blend with p, q still feasible? Not necessarily, but update is heuristic. The theoretical guarantee lost, but line search ensures no worse if accepted. Good.

Maybe add small random perturb to LP direction to escape degeneracy? If no improve, break.

We should limit LP time: In each loop, if time_left < 250 break to leave for mirror/upsample. Use `if time.monotonic() > deadline - 250: break`.

 profiles generation:
```
def _make_profiles(n):
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  pro=[]
  pro.append(np.ones(n))
  pro.append(np.arange(n,0,-1,dtype=float))
  pro.append(np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float))
  for aa,bb in [(1,3),(3,1),(0.5,2),(2,0.5),(0.5,0.5),(2,2),(0.2,3),(3,0.2)]:
     f=np.power(x+1e-6, aa)*np.power(1-x+1e-6, bb)
     pro.append(f)
  for m,eps in [(1,0.3),(2,0.3),(3,0.5)]:
     f=1+eps*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x)
     pro.append(np.maximum(f,1e-6))
  # random smooth
  for _ in range(4):
     N=1
     while N<2*n: N*=2
     c=rng.normal(size=N//2+1)
     # make satisfy Hermitian by ifft real
     f=np.fft.irfft(c, N)[:n]
     f=np.maximum(f + 2.0, 1e-6) # center to positive
     pro.append(f)
  return pro
```
Normalize in callers.

Potential issue: Mirror from uniform may waste time; but may move. We can run mirror on nonuniform first. profiles: dec, beta(1,3), beta(3,1), inc, cos, uniform. Good.

Let's code interpolation function:
```
def _interp_profile(p, n):
   p=_normalize(p)
   if p.size == n: return p
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,p.size)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   q=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
   return _normalize(q)
```

Detailed helper functions:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full_like(a, 1.0/a.size) if a.size else np.ones(1)
    return a/s
```
For empty, not called.

`_direct_score`:
```
def _direct_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 0.01: return float('inf')
    # But if all normalized sum1, sum>0.01. For initial [0]? skip.
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    if b.size==0: return inf
    return float(2*a.size*float(np.max(b))/(s*s))
```
If a sum huge? OK.

`_fft_score` for internal:
```
def _fft_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-15 or a.size==0: return inf
   n=a.size
   N=1
   while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   M=float(np.max(c))
   return float(2*n*M/(s*s))
```
For normalized s=1.

`_fft_conv` similar but assumes normalized? Use s=1:
```
def _fft_conv(a):
   n=a.size; N=1<<(2*n-1).bit_length()? Use int.bit_length.
   while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
   return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
Need N power of two: `N = 1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()`? Python int bit_length: For length L=2n-1, need N next power of two >=L. `N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()` works if L>1. Or while simple. Use while for clarity.

`_softmax_vec`:
```
def _softmax_vec(y):
   y=np.asarray(y,float).ravel()
   if y.size==0: return np.ones(1)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
   y=np.clip(y, -50, 50)
   y=y-y.max()
   e=np.exp(y)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full_like(y,1/y.size)
   return e/s
```

`_fit_y` nested? Could be helper using `_direct_score` for small n:
```
def _fit_softmax(y,n):
   p=_softmax_vec(y)
   p=np.zeros(n); p[:y.size]=p?```
But y length n. Use direct score.

Small search nested:
```
def _small_search():
  if time.monotonic() > deadline - 80: return
  limits = {6:40,5:30,4:20}
  for n in (6,5,4):
    stop = time.monotonic() + limits[n]
    def fit(y):
       y=np.asarray(y,float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): return 1e100
       p=_softmax_vec(y)
       return _direct_score(p)
    starts=[]
    try:
      bounds=[(-6,6)]*n
      res=differential_evolution(fit, bounds, seed=123+n, maxiter=80, popsize=100, polish=False, tol=1e-6)
      starts.append(res.x)
      # Also take res? only best
    except Exception: pass
    for _ in range(20):
         starts.append(rng.normal(size=n)*1.5)
    for y0 in starts:
      if time.monotonic() > stop: break
      try:
        res=minimize(fit, y0, method='Powell', options={'maxiter':800, 'maxfev':8000})
        _consider(res.x) ? Actually need softmax p, so consider _softmax_vec(res.x)
      except:
        _consider(_softmax_vec(y0))
```
But `_consider` expects array. Good. `differential_evolution` can be slow for n=6 popsize100 maxiter80=8000 evals, direct convolve length6 trivial. For n=5,4. 3* maybe 1s.
If time budget reserved for LP, set limits smaller: n=6 15s, n=5 10, n=4 5. Use overall cap 35s.

LP nested with linprog: Need import `linprog`. If LP fails due nonconvergence, handle.
```
def _solve_lp(p):
   n=p.size
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), float)
   for j in range(n): A[j:j+n, j]=p
   M=_fft_conv(p).max()
   rhs=M*np.ones(2*n-1)
   cvec=-np.ones(n)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   for method, opts in [...]:
      try:
         res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=rhs, bounds=bounds, method=method, options=opts)
         if res is not None and res.success and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
             g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
             if np.all(np.isfinite(g)) and g.sum()>1e-12:
                 return np.clip(g,0,1)
      except Exception:
         pass
   return None
```
HiGHS `success` may be True even with optimal. If status 0 success True. Status 3 infeasible? ignore.
`method=None` default. Tuples list: `('highs', {'TimeLimit':30,'output_flag':0, 'presolve': True})`, `('highs', {})`, `(None,{})`. If method None, don't pass? linprog(method=None) allowed? I think method optional; if None, might error? Use `if method is not None: kwargs['method']=method`.

Inside `_run_lp`:
```
def _run_lp(n,p0,iters):
   nonlocal best...
   p=_normalize(p0); local=p.copy(); lsc=_fft_score(p)
   no=0
   for it in range(iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 200: break
       M=_fft_conv(p).max()
       if M <= 0: break
       g=_solve_lp(p)
       if g is None:
           g=p
       else:
           g=0.8*g + 0.2*p
           g=np.clip(g,0,None); gs=g.sum(); if gs>0: g/=gs
       qg=g/g.sum()
       imp=False
       for t in (0.25,0.5,0.1,0.35,0.75,1.0,0.05,0.15):
           pnew=(1-t)*p + t*qg
           sc=_fft_score(pnew)
           if sc < lsc - 1e-13:
               p=pnew; lsc=sc; local=p.copy(); imp=True; break
       if imp:
           no=0
           scd=_direct_score(p); if scd<best_score: update
       else:
           no+=1
           # maybe try qg with more blend? if no>10 break
           if no > 25: break
   scd=_direct_score(local); if scd<best_score update
   return local
```

def _beta_search():
  n_eval=128
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n_eval)
  def f2(v):
     aa,bb=max(0,v)
     eps=1e-6
     p=np.power(x+eps,aa)*np.power(1-x+eps,bb)
     return _direct_score(_normalize(p))
  for start in [(0.5,0.5),(1,3),(3,1),(0.2,1),(1,0.2),(2,4),(4,2)]:
     try: res=minimize(f2,start,method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':500})
     except: continue
     aa,bb=res.x; consider(_beta_profile(512,aa,bb))

def _beta_gradient_search():
  n_eval=128
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n_eval)
  def obj(v):
     aa=max(0.0,float(v[0])); bb=max(0.0,float(v[1]))
     eps=1e-7
     try:
       p=np.power(x+eps,aa)*np.power(1-x+eps,bb)
       return _direct_score(_normalize(p))
     except Exception: return 1e100
  starts=[(0.5,0.5),(1,3),(3,1),(0.2,2),(2,0.2),(1,1),(0.5,2),(2,0.5)]
  for st in starts:
     if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
     try:
       res=minimize(obj, np.array(st,float), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':400, 'xatol':1e-6, 'fatol':1e-10})
       a,b=res.x
       for n_out in (256, 512):
          p=np.power(np.linspace(0,1,n_out)+1e-7,a)*np.power(1-np.linspace(0,1,n_out)+1e-7,b)
          _consider(p)
     except: pass

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np

from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize, differential_evolution


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=float)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.shape, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=float)
    return a / s


def _direct_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(np.max(b)) / (s * s))


def _fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1
    while N < m:
        N <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:m]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _fft_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1
    while N < m:
        N <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:m]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    mm = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mm / (s * s))


def _softmax_vec(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).ravel()
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=float)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = np.clip(y, -50.0, 50.0)
    y = y - y.max()
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(y.shape, 1.0 / y.size, dtype=float)
    return e / s


def _interp_profile(p, n):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if p.size == n:
        return p.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return _normalize(q)


def _beta_profile(n, aa, bb):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    eps = 1e-8
    aa = max(0.0, float(aa))
    bb = max(0.0, float(bb))
    try:
        return np.power(x + eps, aa) * np.power(1.0 - x + eps, bb)
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    budget_s = float(max(1.0, budget_s))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, budget_s - 10.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_list = np.asarray([1.0, 2.0], dtype=float)
    best_score = _direct_score(best_list)

    def tl():
        return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    def consider(a):
        nonlocal best_list, best_score
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return
        a = a / s
        sc = _direct_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_list = a.astype(float, copy=True)

    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            consider(parent)
        except Exception:
            pass

    for cand in ([1.0, 2.0], [1.0, 1.0, 2.0, 1.0], [1.0, 2.0, 1.0], [1.0, 1.0, 1.0]):
        consider(cand)

    def small_search():
        if tl() < 80.0:
            return
        per_n = {6: 22.0, 5: 14.0, 4: 8.0}
        for n in (6, 5, 4):
            stop = time.monotonic() + min(per_n.get(n, 10.0), max(1.0, tl() - 90.0))
            if time.monotonic() > stop:
                continue

            def fit(y):
                y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
                    return 1e100
                p = _softmax_vec(y)
                return _direct_score(p)

            starts = [np.zeros(n, dtype=float)]
            try:
                res = differential_evolution(
                    fit,
                    [(-6.0, 6.0)] * n,
                    seed=123 + n,
                    maxiter=70,
                    popsize=90,
                    tol=1e-6,
                    polish=False,
                )
                if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
                    starts.append(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float))
            except Exception:
                pass

            for _ in range(18):
                starts.append(rng.normal(size=n, scale=1.4))

            for y0 in starts:
                if time.monotonic() > stop:
                    break
                y0 = np.asarray(y0, dtype=float)
                consider(_softmax_vec(y0))
                try:
                    res = minimize(fit, y0, method="Powell",
                                   options={"maxiter": 900, "maxfev": 9000})
                    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
                        consider(_softmax_vec(res.x))
                except Exception:
                    pass

    def beta_search():
        if tl() < 80.0:
            return
        n_eval = 128
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_eval)

        def obj(v):
            if len(v) != 2:
                return 1e100
            return _direct_score(_normalize(_beta_profile(n_eval, v[0], v[1])))

        starts = [(0.5, 0.5), (1.0, 3.0), (3.0, 1.0), (0.2, 2.0),
                  (2.0, 0.2), (0.5, 2.0), (2.0, 0.5), (1.0, 1.0)]
        for st in starts:
            if tl() < 60.0:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(obj, np.asarray(st, dtype=float), method="Nelder-Mead",
                               options={"maxiter": 500, "xatol": 1e-7, "fatol": 1e-11})
                if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x") or res.x is None:
                    continue
                aa, bb = float(res.x[0]), float(res.x[1])
                for n_out in (256, 512):
                    consider(_beta_profile(n_out, aa, bb))
            except Exception:
                pass

    small_search()
    beta_search()

    def make_profiles(n):
        n = int(max(4, n))
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        profs = []
        profs.append(np.ones(n, dtype=float))
        profs.append(np.arange(n, 0, -1, dtype=float))
        profs.append(np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=float))
        for aa, bb in [(1.0, 3.0), (3.0, 1.0), (0.5, 2.0), (2.0, 0.5),
                       (0.5, 0.5), (2.0, 2.0), (0.2, 3.0), (3.0, 0.2)]:
            profs.append(_beta_profile(n, aa, bb))
        for mfr, amp in [(1, 0.25), (2, 0.35), (3, 0.50)]:
            profs.append(np.maximum(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * mfr * x), 1e-6))
        for _ in range(4):
            N = 1
            while N < 2 * n:
                N <<= 1
            coeff = rng.normal(size=N // 2 + 1)
            f = np.fft.irfft(coeff, N)[:n]
            f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
            f = np.maximum(3.0 + f, 1e-6)
            profs.append(f)
        for i, p in enumerate(profs):
            profs[i] = _normalize(p)
        # Prefer non-uniform starts first.
        order = list(range(len(profs)))
        uniform_idx = 0
        if uniform_idx in order:
            order.remove(uniform_idx)
            order.append(uniform_idx)
        return [profs[i] for i in order]

    def mirror_optimize(n, p0, iters):
        nonlocal best_list, best_score
        p = _normalize(p0)
        local = p.copy()
        local_sc = _fft_score(local)
        scale = 0.35
        no_improve = 0

        for _ in range(int(max(0, iters))):
            if tl() < 80.0:
                break
            c = _fft_conv(p)
            mm = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
            if mm <= 0.0:
                break

            K = min(c.size, max(32, int(n // 8)))
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
            beta = 20.0 / max(mm, 1e-15)
            z = beta * (c[idx] - mm)
            w = np.exp(z)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 1e-15:
                w = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / idx.size)
            else:
                w = w / ws

            g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            for wk, k in zip(w, idx):
                if wk <= 1e-7:
                    continue
                k = int(k)
                lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
                hi = min(n - 1, k)
                if lo > hi:
                    continue
                g[lo:hi + 1] += 2.0 * wk * p[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]

            gm = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
            if gm <= 1e-15:
                break
            r = g / gm
            improved = False

            for eta in (0.1 * scale, 0.3 * scale, 1.0 * scale, 3.0 * scale, 10.0 * scale):
                eta = float(min(50.0, max(1e-3, eta)))
                try:
                    q = p * np.exp(-eta * r)
                    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
                    q = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
                    qs = float(q.sum())
                    if qs <= 1e-12:
                        continue
                    q /= qs
                    sc = _fft_score(q)
                    if sc < local_sc - 1e-13:
                        local = q
                        local_sc = sc
                        p = q
                        improved = True
                        break
                except Exception:
                    continue

            if not improved:
                base = float(p.mean()) / gm
                for coeff in (0.1 * scale, 0.4 * scale, 1.5 * scale, 6.0 * scale):
                    coeff = float(min(100.0, max(1e-3, coeff)))
                    try:
                        q = p - coeff * base * g
                        q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
                        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
                        qs = float(q.sum())
                        if qs <= 1e-12:
                            continue
                        q /= qs
                        sc = _fft_score(q)
                        if sc < local_sc - 1e-13:
                            local = q
                            local_sc = sc
                            p = q
                            improved = True
                            break
                    except Exception:
                        continue

            if improved:
                no_improve = 0
                scale = min(10.0, scale * 1.12)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                scale = max(0.02, scale * 0.60)
                if no_improve > 250:
                    break

        try:
            sc = _direct_score(local)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_list = local.astype(float, copy=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def solve_lp(p):
        n = int(p.size)
        if n < 2:
            return None
        m = 2 * n - 1
        A = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=float)
        # Column j: A[i+j, j] = p[i]
        for j in range(n):
            A[j:j + n, j] = p
        mm = float(np.max(_fft_conv(p))) * 1.001 + 1e-15
        rhs = mm * np.ones(m, dtype=float)
        cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

        attempts = [
            ("highs", {"TimeLimit": int(max(2, min(10, tl() - 60))), "output_flag": 0}),
            ("highs", {}),
            (None, {}),
        ]
        last_err = None
        for meth, opts in attempts:
            try:
                kw = {
                    "A_ub": A,
                    "b_ub": rhs,
                    "bounds": bounds,
                }
                if opts:
                    kw["options"] = opts
                if meth is not None:
                    kw["method"] = meth
                res = linprog(cvec, **kw)
                if res is None:
                    continue
                if getattr(res, "success", False) or getattr(res, "status", -1) == 0:
                    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
                    if x is not None:
                        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
                        if x.shape == (n,) and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
                            if float(x.sum()) > 1e-12:
                                return x
                last_err = "no solution"
            except Exception as e:
                last_err = repr(e)
                continue
        if last_err is not None:
            # Store nowhere; silent fallback.
            pass
        return None

    def run_lp(n, p0, iters):
        nonlocal best_list, best_score
        p = _normalize(p0)
        local = p.copy()
        local_sc = _fft_score(local)
        no_improve = 0
        failures = 0

        for _ in range(int(max(0, iters))):
            if tl() < 180.0:
                break
            mm = float(np.max(_fft_conv(p)))
            if mm <= 0.0:
                break

            g = solve_lp(p)
            if g is None:
                failures += 1
                if failures >= 4:
                    break
                g = p
            else:
                failures = 0
                # Break degenerate LP solutions and keep a stable direction.
                g = 0.80 * g + 0.20 * p
                g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
                g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
                gs = float(g.sum())
                if gs <= 1e-12:
                    g = p
                    gs = float(p.sum())
                g = g / gs

            q = g / max(1e-15, float(g.sum()))
            improved = False
            for t in (0.25, 0.50, 0.10, 0.35, 0.75, 1.00, 0.05, 0.15):
                try:
                    pnew = (1.0 - t) * p + t * q
                    sc = _fft_score(pnew)
                    if sc < local_sc - 1e-13:
                        p = pnew
                        local = pnew.copy()
                        local_sc = sc
                        improved = True
                        break
                except Exception:
                    continue

            if improved:
                no_improve = 0
                try:
                    scd = _direct_score(local)
                    if np.isfinite(scd) and scd < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = float(scd)
                        best_list = local.astype(float, copy=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve >= 20:
                    break

        try:
            scd = _direct_score(local)
            if np.isfinite(scd) and scd < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = float(scd)
                best_list = local.astype(float, copy=True)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return local

    # Progressive optimization at moderate lengths.
    n1 = 512
    if tl() > 180:
        profiles = make_profiles(n1)[:7]
        for prof in profiles:
            if tl() < 140:
                break
            mirror_optimize(n1, prof, 220)

    if tl() > 220:
        p_start = best_list if best_list.size == n1 else _interp_profile(best_list, n1)
        run_lp(n1, p_start, 70)

    if tl() > 180:
        p_start = _interp_profile(best_list, n1)
        mirror_optimize(n1, p_start, 350)

    if tl() > 260:
        n2 = 1024
        p_start = _interp_profile(best_list, n2)
        run_lp(n2, p_start, 45)
        if tl() > 140:
            p_start = _interp_profile(best_list, n2)
            mirror_optimize(n2, p_start, 350)

    if tl() > 200:
        n3 = 2048
        p_start = _interp_profile(best_list, n3)
        mirror_optimize(n3, p_start, 700)

    if tl() > 120:
        # A final modest polish on the best available finite-size candidate.
        nz = max(64, min(2048, int(best_list.size)))
        p_start = _interp_profile(best_list, nz)
        mirror_optimize(nz, p_start, 300)

    def finalize(seq):
        try:
            seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(seq, dtype=float).ravel()]
        except Exception:
            return None
        if not seq:
            return None
        for x in seq:
            if not math.isfinite(x):
                return None
        return seq

    cand = finalize(best_list)
    if cand is None:
        cand = finalize([1.0, 2.0])
    if cand is None:
        cand = [1.0]

    try:
        sc = evaluate_sequence(cand)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            cand = finalize([1.0, 2.0])
            sc = evaluate_sequence(cand)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                cand = [1.0]
    except Exception:
        cand = [1.0]

    return cand
```
